    PROCEDURE ItemChargeAssignment@8053004(PurchLine_P@8053000 : Record 39);
    VAR
      ItemChargeAssignment_L@8053004 : Record 5805;
      NextLineNo_L@8053003 : Integer;
      Currency_L@8053002 : Record 4;
      QtyToAssign_L@8053001 : Decimal;
      PurchLine_L@8053005 : Record 39;
      ItemChargeAssgntPurchCU_L@8053006 : Codeunit 5805;
      PurchHeader_L@8053007 : Record 38;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20130718-Nut08-CMC02.02.00-WSA02

      CLEAR(ItemChargeAssignment_L);

      ItemChargeAssignment_L.RESET;
      ItemChargeAssignment_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
      ItemChargeAssignment_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",PurchLine_P."Document No.");
      ItemChargeAssignment_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",PurchLine_P."Line No.");
      IF ItemChargeAssignment_L.ISEMPTY THEN
       BEGIN
        PurchLine_L.GET(PurchLine_P."Document Type",PurchLine_P."Document No.",PurchLine_P."Item Charge Root Line No.");

        ItemChargeAssignment_L.INIT;
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Document Type" := PurchLine_P."Document Type";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Document No." := PurchLine_P."Document No.";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Document Line No." := PurchLine_P."Line No.";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Line No." := 10000;
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Item Charge No." := PurchLine_P."No.";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Applies-to Doc. Type" := PurchLine_P."Document Type";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Applies-to Doc. No." := PurchLine_P."Document No.";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Applies-to Doc. Line No." := PurchLine_L."Line No.";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Item No." := PurchLine_L."No.";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L.Description := PurchLine_L.Description;

        IF "Currency Code" = '' THEN
          Currency_L.InitRoundingPrecision
        ELSE
         BEGIN
          PurchHeader_L.GET(PurchLine_P."Document Type",PurchLine_P."Document No.");
          PurchHeader_L.TESTFIELD("Currency Factor");
          Currency_L.GET("Currency Code");
          Currency_L.TESTFIELD("Amount Rounding Precision");
         END;
        ItemChargeAssignment_L."Unit Cost" := PurchLine_P."Unit Cost";
        ItemChargeAssignment_L.INSERT;
       END;

      PurchLine_P.CALCFIELDS("Qty. Assigned");
      // --> 20170221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23844-JCD01
      {BEFORE
      QtyToAssign_L := ( PurchLine_P."Qty. to Receive" + PurchLine_P."Quantity Received" ) - PurchLine_P."Qty. Assigned";
      ItemChargeAssgntPurchCU_L.SuggestAssgnt2(PurchLine_P,QtyToAssign_L,1);
      AFTER}
      IF PurchLine_P."Document Type" IN [PurchLine_P."Document Type"::"Credit Memo",PurchLine_P."Document Type"::"Return Order"] THEN
      BEGIN
        QtyToAssign_L := ( PurchLine_P."Return Qty. to Ship" + PurchLine_P."Return Qty. Shipped" )
           - PurchLine_P."Qty. Assigned";
        ItemChargeAssgntPurchCU_L.SuggestAssgnt2(PurchLine_P,QtyToAssign_L,1);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        QtyToAssign_L := ( PurchLine_P."Qty. to Receive" + PurchLine_P."Quantity Received" ) - PurchLine_P."Qty. Assigned";
        ItemChargeAssgntPurchCU_L.SuggestAssgnt2(PurchLine_P,QtyToAssign_L,1);
      END;
      // <-- 20170221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23844-JCD01

      // <-- 20130718-Nut08-CMC02.02.00-WSA02
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetFromDirectShip@8053005(NewFromDirectShip_P@8053000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      FromDirectShip_G := NewFromDirectShip_P;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalculateBugetedUnitCost@8053006() : Boolean;
    VAR
      CMCPurchDisc_L@8053000 : Record 8053247;
      CMCPurchDiscValue_L@8053001 : Record 8053248;
      CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L@8053002 : TEMPORARY Record 8053249;
      CMCPurchDocDisc_L@8053006 : Record 8053249;
      PurchaseHeader_L@8053003 : Record 38;
      Item_L@8053005 : Record 27;
      j_L@8053007 : Integer;
      i_L@8053004 : Integer;
      PreviousFinalPrice_L@8053009 : Decimal;
      TotalDiscount_L@8053008 : Decimal;
      CMCSetup_L@8053010 : Record 8052982;
      DiscountUnitAmount_L@8053011 : Decimal;
      RecECDLedgerEntry_L@1000000000 : Record 8053088;
      RecECDValueEntryDiscount_L@1000000001 : Record 8053252;
      BooIsExctracted_L@1000000002 : Boolean;
      BooRfcExist_L@1000000003 : Boolean;
      RecRcpLine_L@1000000004 : Record 121;
      RecPurchHeader_L@1000000005 : Record 38;
      RecReturnShipmentLine_L@1000000006 : Record 6651;
    BEGIN
      //-->20130806-APP33-CMC02.02.00-PPE01
      // --> 20141023-WI00-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
      IF Type = Type::Item THEN BEGIN
        Item_L.GET("No.");
        Item_L.CALCFIELDS("Cereal Item");
        IF Item_L."Cereal Item" THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);
      END;
      // <-- 20141023-WI00-CMC02.02.00-FHS01

      IF (Type <> Type::Item) OR NotUpdateECD_G THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      // --> 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
      FctSetfilterAndTest(BooIsExctracted_L,BooRfcExist_L,RecECDLedgerEntry_L);

      IF BooIsExctracted_L AND BooRfcExist_L THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT CMCPurchDisc_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
              UpdateECDUnitAmount;
              EXIT(FALSE);
            END;

          PurchaseHeader_L.GET("Document Type", "Document No.");

          CMCPurchDocDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", "Document Type");
          CMCPurchDocDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", "Document No.");
          CMCPurchDocDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", "Line No.");
          IF NOT CMCPurchDocDisc_L.ISEMPTY THEN
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);
          CMCPurchDocDisc_L.RESET;

          IF ("Direct Unit Cost" = 0) OR ("Line Discount %" = 100) THEN BEGIN
            "ECD Unit Amount" := 0;
            VALIDATE("Budgeted Unit Cost", "Direct Unit Cost");
            EXIT(FALSE);
          END;


          //Gestion compl'tement nouvelle dans le cas de la cr'ation d'une facture par extraction de ligne BR
          //on ne va pas voir s'il y a des RFC param'tr' mais on utilise les RFC g'n'r'es sur la ligne de Reception
          //on utilise notre num'ro BR pour trouver nos lignes RFC
          CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.RESET;
          CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.DELETEALL;

          //Les filtres sur RecECDLedgerEntry_L ont 't' pos's lors de l'utilisation de la fonction FctSetfilterAndTest
          IF RecECDLedgerEntry_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.INIT;
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Document Type", "Document Type");
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Document No.", "Document No.");
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Document Line No.", "Line No.");
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount Code", RecECDLedgerEntry_L."Discount Code");
              //On a besoin d'info dans la table "CMC Value Entry Discount"
              RecECDValueEntryDiscount_L.GET(RecECDLedgerEntry_L."Value Entry No.",RecECDLedgerEntry_L."Discount Code");
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE(Rank, RecECDValueEntryDiscount_L.Rank);
              IF Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                TotalDiscount_L := ROUND("Line Discount Amount" / ABS(Quantity), 0.00001);
                CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Base Value", "Direct Unit Cost" - TotalDiscount_L);
              END ELSE
                CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Base Value", "Direct Unit Cost");


              CASE CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Valuation Type" OF
                CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Valuation Type"::Percentage:
                  BEGIN
                    CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount %", RecECDValueEntryDiscount_L."Discount %");
                    CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount Amount",
                               ROUND(RecECDValueEntryDiscount_L."Discount Amount", 0.01, '='));

                  END;
                CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Valuation Type"::"Unit Amount":
                  BEGIN
                    CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount Amount", RecECDLedgerEntry_L."Cost per Unit" -
                                                                      RecECDLedgerEntry_L."Net Unit Price (Receipt)");
                  END;
              END;
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L."ECD Vendor No." := RecECDValueEntryDiscount_L."ECD Vendor No.";
              // --> 20170126-WI00-ADV01.01.00-24651-CJY01
              {BEFORE
              CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.INSERT(TRUE);
              AFTER}
              IF CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.INSERT(TRUE) THEN;
              // <-- 20170126-WI00-ADV01.01.00-24651-CJY01
            UNTIL RecECDLedgerEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END ELSE BEGIN
      // <-- 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
        IF NOT CMCPurchDisc_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
            UpdateECDUnitAmount;
            EXIT(FALSE);
          END;

        PurchaseHeader_L.GET("Document Type", "Document No.");

        CMCPurchDocDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", "Document Type");
        CMCPurchDocDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", "Document No.");
        CMCPurchDocDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", "Line No.");
        IF NOT CMCPurchDocDisc_L.ISEMPTY THEN
          CMCPurchDocDisc_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);
        CMCPurchDocDisc_L.RESET;

        IF ("Direct Unit Cost" = 0) OR ("Line Discount %" = 100) THEN BEGIN
          "ECD Unit Amount" := 0;
          VALIDATE("Budgeted Unit Cost", "Direct Unit Cost");
          EXIT(FALSE);
        END;

        CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.RESET;
        CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.DELETEALL;

        REPEAT
          FOR j_L := 0 TO 2 DO BEGIN
            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.RESET;
            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE("Purch. Discount Code", CMCPurchDisc_L.Code);
            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Campaign No.",'%1|%2',"CMC Campaign No.",'');
            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE("Vendor Type",j_L);
            // --> 20160928-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3106-CJY01
            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Discount Value",'<>%1',0);
            // <-- 20160928-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3106-CJY01


            CASE j_L OF
              0 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE("Vendor No.", "Buy-from Vendor No.");
              1 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE("Vendor No.", "Pay-to Vendor No.");
              2 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE("Vendor No.", "Origin Vendor No.");
            END;
                // --> 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
                {BEFORE
                IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Blanket Order" THEN BEGIN
                AFTER}
                IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Blanket Order") OR
                   ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice) OR
                   ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo")
                  THEN BEGIN
                    IF NOT BooIsExctracted_L THEN BEGIN
                      // <-- 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
                      CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1', PurchaseHeader_L."Document Date");
                      CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>=%1', PurchaseHeader_L."Document Date");
                      // --> 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      //Si nous sommes ici, c'est que noous sommes li' . une commande
                      //recherche date commande pour appliquer les RFC
                      IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
                        //Grace au num'ro de BR on remonte . la commande
                        IF RecRcpLine_L.GET("Receipt No.","Receipt Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                          IF RecPurchHeader_L.GET(RecPurchHeader_L."Document Type"::Order,RecRcpLine_L."Order No.") THEN BEGIN
                            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1', RecPurchHeader_L."Order Date");
                            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>=%1', RecPurchHeader_L."Order Date");
                          END;
                        END;
                      END;
                      IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo" THEN BEGIN
                        //Grace au num'ro de Return Shipment on remonte au retour
                        IF RecReturnShipmentLine_L.GET("Return Shipment No.","Return Shipment Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                          IF RecPurchHeader_L.GET(RecPurchHeader_L."Document Type"::"Return Order" ,
                            RecReturnShipmentLine_L."Return Order No.")
                          THEN BEGIN
                            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1', RecPurchHeader_L."Order Date");
                            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>=%1', RecPurchHeader_L."Order Date");
                          END;
                        END;
                      END;

                END;
                // <-- 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
            END ELSE BEGIN
              CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1', PurchaseHeader_L."Order Date");
              CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>=%1', PurchaseHeader_L."Order Date");
            END;


            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Blanket Order No.",'%1|%2',"Blanket Order No.",'');
            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Minimum Quantity",'<=%1|0',Quantity);
            CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Unit of Measure Code",'%1|%2',"Unit of Measure Code",'');

              IF CMCPurchDiscValue_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                FOR i_L := 0 TO 5 DO BEGIN
                CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE(Type, i_L);
                CASE i_L OF
                    0 :
                      BEGIN
                    CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE(Code, "No.");
                    CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETFILTER("Variant Code",'%1|%2',"Variant Code",'');
                  END;
                  1 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE(Code, Item."Item Disc. Group");
                  2 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE(Code, "Species Code");
                  3 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE(Code, "Sub-Family Code");
                  4 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE(Code, "Family Code");
                  5 : CMCPurchDiscValue_L.SETRANGE(Code, "Activity Code");
                END;

                  IF CMCPurchDiscValue_L.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                  j_L := 2;
                  i_L := 5;
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.INIT;
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Document Type", "Document Type");
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Document No.", "Document No.");
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Document Line No.", "Line No.");
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount Code", CMCPurchDisc_L.Code);
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE(Rank, CMCPurchDisc_L.Rank);

                  IF Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                    TotalDiscount_L := ROUND("Line Discount Amount" / ABS(Quantity), 0.00001);
                    CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Base Value", "Direct Unit Cost" - TotalDiscount_L);
                  END ELSE
                    CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Base Value", "Direct Unit Cost");

                  CASE CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Valuation Type" OF
                    CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Valuation Type"::Percentage:
                      BEGIN
                        CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount %", CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Discount Value");
                        CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount Amount",
                                   ROUND(CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L."Base Value" * CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L."Discount %" /100, 0.01, '='));
                      END;
                    CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Valuation Type"::"Unit Amount":
                      BEGIN
                        CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.VALIDATE("Discount Amount", CMCPurchDiscValue_L."Discount Value");
                      END;
                  END;
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L."ECD Vendor No." := CMCPurchDiscValue_L."ECD Vendor No.";
                  // --> 20170126-WI00-ADV01.01.00-24651-CJY01
                  {BEFORE
                  CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.INSERT(TRUE);
                  AFTER}
                  IF CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.INSERT(TRUE) THEN;
                  // <-- 20170126-WI00-ADV01.01.00-24651-CJY01
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL CMCPurchDisc_L.NEXT = 0;
      // --> 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
      END;
      // <-- 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01


      CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Rank);

      IF NOT CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        CASE "ECD Calculation Priority" OF
          "ECD Calculation Priority"::"ECD Value" :
            "ECD Unit Amount" := 0;
        END;
        UpdateECDUnitAmount;
        EXIT(FALSE);
      END;

      CMCPurchDocDisc_L.INIT;
      CMCPurchDocDisc_L := CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L;
      CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Final Price" := CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Base Value" - CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Discount Amount";
      CMCPurchDocDisc_L.INSERT(TRUE);

      PreviousFinalPrice_L := CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Final Price";

      TotalDiscount_L += CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Discount Amount";

      IF CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.NEXT <> 0  THEN
      REPEAT
        CMCPurchDocDisc_L.INIT;
        CMCPurchDocDisc_L := CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L;

        CMCPurchDisc_L.GET(CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L."Discount Code");

        CASE CMCPurchDisc_L."Disc. Calculation Type" OF
          CMCPurchDisc_L."Disc. Calculation Type"::"Sum Discount":
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Base Value", CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L."Base Value");
          CMCPurchDisc_L."Disc. Calculation Type"::"Chain Discount":
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Base Value", PreviousFinalPrice_L);
        END;

        IF CMCPurchDisc_L."Valuation Type" = CMCPurchDisc_L."Valuation Type"::Percentage THEN
          CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Discount Amount",
                                     ROUND(CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Base Value" * CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Discount %" /100, 0.01, '='));

        CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Final Price", PreviousFinalPrice_L - CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Discount Amount");
        CMCPurchDocDisc_L.INSERT(TRUE);

        PreviousFinalPrice_L := CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Final Price";
        TotalDiscount_L += CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Discount Amount";

      UNTIL CMCPurchDocDiscTemp_L.NEXT = 0;

      CASE "ECD Calculation Priority" OF
        "ECD Calculation Priority"::"Budgeted Unit Cost" :
          BEGIN
          CMCSetup_L.GET;
          CMCSetup_L.TESTFIELD(CMCSetup_L."Except. Purch. ECD Disc. Code");
          IF CMCPurchDisc_L.GET(CMCSetup_L."Except. Purch. ECD Disc. Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF CMCPurchDisc_L.Rank <= CMCPurchDocDisc_L.Rank THEN
              ERROR(Text8053013,FORMAT(CMCPurchDisc_L.Code),FORMAT(CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Discount Code"));
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.INIT;
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Document Type", "Document Type");
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Document No.", "Document No.");
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Document Line No.", "Line No.");
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Discount Code", CMCSetup_L."Except. Purch. ECD Disc. Code");
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE(Rank, CMCPurchDisc_L.Rank);
            CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Base Value", PreviousFinalPrice_L);
            IF CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Base Value" > "Budgeted Unit Cost" THEN BEGIN
              CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Discount Amount",CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Base Value" - "Budgeted Unit Cost");
              CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("ECD Vendor No.","ECD Vendor No.");
              CMCPurchDocDisc_L.VALIDATE("Final Price", CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Base Value" - CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Discount Amount");
              CMCPurchDocDisc_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            END ELSE
              IF CMCPurchDocDisc_L."Base Value" < "Budgeted Unit Cost" THEN
                ERROR(Text8053014,CMCPurchDocDisc_L.FIELDCAPTION("Discount Amount"),
                                  CMCPurchDocDisc_L.FIELDCAPTION("Discount Code"),
                                  FORMAT(CMCSetup_L."Except. Purch. ECD Disc. Code"),
                                  CMCPurchDocDisc_L.TABLECAPTION);
          END ELSE
            ERROR(Text8053012,CMCSetup_L."Except. Purch. ECD Disc. Code");
        END;
        "ECD Calculation Priority"::"ECD Value" :
          BEGIN
          IF CurrFieldNo <> "Budgeted Unit Cost" THEN BEGIN
            IF Quantity = 0 THEN
              DiscountUnitAmount_L := 0
            ELSE
              DiscountUnitAmount_L :=
                ROUND("Line Discount Amount" / Quantity,0.00001);
            "ECD Unit Amount" := TotalDiscount_L - DiscountUnitAmount_L;
            "Budgeted Unit Cost" := "Direct Unit Cost" - DiscountUnitAmount_L - "ECD Unit Amount";
          END;
        END;
      END;

      UpdateECDUnitAmount;

      IF TotalDiscount_L - DiscountUnitAmount_L > 0 THEN
        EXIT(TRUE)
      ELSE
        EXIT(FALSE);
      //<--20130806-APP33-CMC02.02.00-PPE01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateQteOfPurchline@8053048(Quantity_P@8053001 : Decimal;QuantityType_P@8053004 : 'Qte,Qte to receive,Qte to invoice');
    VAR
      PurchLineKit_L@8053005 : Record 39;

            VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity_P);
         END;
        QuantityType_P::"Qte to receive" :

          END;
        QuantityType_P::"Qte to invoice" :
         BEGIN
            VALIDATE("Qty. to Invoice", Quantity_P);
          END;
      END;
      // --> 20131210-Nut08-CMC02.02.00-1226-WSA01
      //BEFORE
      //MODIFY(TRUE);
      //AFTER
      MODIFY;
      // <-- 20131210-Nut08-CMC02.02.00-1226-WSA01
      // <-- 20130923-Nut08-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
    END;

    PROCEDURE Setcallfromsales@8053007(CallfromSales_P@8053000 : Boolean);

    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateQteKitPurchLine@8053008(Quantity_P@8053001 : Decimal;QuantityType_P@8053000 : 'Qte,Qte to receive,Qte to invoice,Return Qty. to Ship');
    VAR
      PurchLineKit_L@8053005 : Record 39;
      UnitCost_L@8053004 : Decimal;
      Rate_L@8053003 : Decimal;
      LineDiscount_L@8053002 : Decimal;
      SumQty_L@1000000000 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // mise . jour lignes frais annexes
        PurchLineKit_L.RESET;
        PurchLineKit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.");
        PurchLineKit_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
        PurchLineKit_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
        PurchLineKit_L.SETRANGE("Item Charge Root Line No.","Line No.");
      IF PurchLineKit_L.FINDSET( TRUE , FALSE ) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          UnitCost_L := PurchLineKit_L."Direct Unit Cost";
          Rate_L := PurchLineKit_L."Line Amount"/PurchLineKit_L.Quantity;
          PurchLineKit_L.SetFromDirectShip(TRUE);
          CASE QuantityType_P OF
            QuantityType_P::Qte :
              PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,ROUND(Quantity_P * PurchLineKit_L."Item Charge Quantity Per" ,0.00001));
            QuantityType_P::"Qte to receive" :
              // --> 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
              IF "Qty. to Receive" + "Quantity Received" = Quantity  THEN
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive",PurchLineKit_L."Outstanding Quantity")
              ELSE
              // <-- 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
              PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive",ROUND(Quantity_P * PurchLineKit_L."Item Charge Quantity Per" ,0.00001));
            QuantityType_P::"Qte to invoice" :
              // --> 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
              IF "Qty. to Invoice" + "Quantity Invoiced" = Quantity  THEN
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Invoice",PurchLineKit_L.Quantity - PurchLineKit_L."Quantity Invoiced")
              ELSE
              // <-- 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
              PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Invoice",ROUND(Quantity_P * PurchLineKit_L."Item Charge Quantity Per" ,0.00001));
            // --> 20170221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23844-JCD01
            QuantityType_P::"Return Qty. to Ship" :
              IF "Return Qty. to Ship" + "Return Qty. Shipped" = Quantity  THEN
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Return Qty. to Ship",PurchLineKit_L."Outstanding Quantity")
              ELSE
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Return Qty. to Ship",ROUND(Quantity_P * PurchLineKit_L."Item Charge Quantity Per" ,0.00001));
            // <-- 20170221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23844-JCD01
          END;
          // --> 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
          SumQty_L += ROUND(Quantity_P * PurchLineKit_L."Item Charge Quantity Per" ,0.00001);
          // <-- 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
          PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Direct Unit Cost",UnitCost_L);
          PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Line Amount",Rate_L*PurchLineKit_L.Quantity);
          PurchLineKit_L.MODIFY;
          PurchLineKit_L.ItemChargeAssignment(PurchLineKit_L);
        UNTIL PurchLineKit_L.NEXT = 0;
        // --> 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
        // On r'partit l''cart d'arrondi sur la deniSre ligne de frais annexe
        IF SumQty_L <> Quantity_P THEN BEGIN
          CASE QuantityType_P OF
            QuantityType_P::Qte :
              PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,PurchLineKit_L.Quantity + (Quantity_P-SumQty_L));
            QuantityType_P::"Qte to receive" :
              IF "Qty. to Receive" + "Quantity Received" = Quantity  THEN
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive",PurchLineKit_L."Outstanding Quantity")
              ELSE
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive",PurchLineKit_L."Qty. to Receive" + (Quantity_P-SumQty_L));
            QuantityType_P::"Qte to invoice" :
              IF "Qty. to Invoice" + "Quantity Invoiced" = Quantity  THEN
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Invoice",PurchLineKit_L.Quantity - PurchLineKit_L."Quantity Invoiced")
              ELSE
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Qty. to Invoice",PurchLineKit_L."Qty. to Invoice" + (Quantity_P-SumQty_L));
            // --> 20170221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23844-JCD01
            QuantityType_P::"Return Qty. to Ship" :
              IF "Return Qty. to Ship" + "Return Qty. Shipped" = Quantity  THEN
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Return Qty. to Ship",PurchLineKit_L."Outstanding Quantity")
              ELSE
                PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Return Qty. to Ship",PurchLineKit_L."Return Qty. to Ship" + (Quantity_P-SumQty_L));
            // <-- 20170221-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23844-JCD01
          END;
          PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Direct Unit Cost",UnitCost_L);
          PurchLineKit_L.VALIDATE("Line Amount",Rate_L * PurchLineKit_L.Quantity);
          PurchLineKit_L.MODIFY;
          PurchLineKit_L.ItemChargeAssignment(PurchLineKit_L);
        END;
        // <-- 20150720-NUT08-ADV01.01.00-1973-DRN01
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemVariety@60000() ItemVariety_R : Code[20];
    VAR
      "<<-- 3LI LOCALS -->>"@60003 : Integer;
      Item_L@60002 : Record 27;
      VarietySetup_L@60001 : Record 60001;
      MultipleFilter_L@60000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // -->20140724-APP08-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // To return the item variety of the Variety code that the item as
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Input:
      //   -
      // Output:
      //   - Code(20): return the item no. variety
      //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


      ItemVariety_R := '';
      IF Type <> Type::Item THEN
        EXIT(ItemVariety_R);


      IF Item_L.GET("No.") THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(VarietySetup_L);
        VarietySetup_L.SETRANGE("Variety Code",Item_L."Variety Code");
        IF (VarietySetup_L.FINDFIRST) AND (VarietySetup_L."Variety Item No." <> '') THEN
          ItemVariety_R := VarietySetup_L."Variety Item No.";
      END;
      EXIT(ItemVariety_R);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateECDUnitAmount@8053014();
    VAR
      DiscountUnitAmount_L@8053000 : Decimal;
      RecItem_L@8053001 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140808-APP33-CMC02.02.00-FDA01

      IF (Type<>Type::Item) THEN EXIT;
      // --> 20141001-WI00-CER02.02.00-2513-BSI01
      RecItem_L.GET("No.");
      RecItem_L.CALCFIELDS("Cereal Item");
      IF RecItem_L."Cereal Item" THEN
        EXIT;
      // <-- 20141001-WI00-CER02.02.00-2513-BSI01
      GetPurchHeader;
      GetGLSetup;
      IF Quantity = 0 THEN
        DiscountUnitAmount_L := 0
      ELSE
        DiscountUnitAmount_L :=
          ROUND("Line Discount Amount" / Quantity,
            GLSetup."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");

      CASE "ECD Calculation Priority" OF
        "ECD Calculation Priority"::"ECD Value" : BEGIN
          IF (CurrFieldNo <> FIELDNO("Budgeted Unit Cost")) THEN BEGIN
            "Budgeted Unit Cost" := "Direct Unit Cost" - DiscountUnitAmount_L - "ECD Unit Amount";
            IF "Budgeted Unit Cost" < 0 THEN
              FIELDERROR("Budgeted Unit Cost",Text029);
          END;
        END;
        "ECD Calculation Priority"::"Budgeted Unit Cost" : BEGIN
          "ECD Unit Amount" := "Direct Unit Cost" - DiscountUnitAmount_L - "Budgeted Unit Cost";
          IF "ECD Unit Amount" < 0 THEN
            FIELDERROR("ECD Unit Amount",Text029);
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20140808-APP33-CMC02.02.00-FDA01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetQtyOnPurchaseBlanketOrder@60001(DocNo_P@60006 : Code[20];LineNo_P@60007 : Integer) QtdOnBlanketOrder_R : Decimal;
    VAR
      "<<-- 3LI LOCALS -->>"@60005 : Integer;
      recItem_L@60004 : Record 27;
      recUnitUOM_L@60003 : Record 5404;
      recCMCUnitsUOM_L@60002 : Record 8053025;
      recPurchLine_L@60001 : Record 39;
      QtdReceived_L@60000 : Decimal;
      QtyPerUOM_L@60008 : Decimal;
      recBlanketPurchLine_L@60009 : Record 39;
      recCMCUnitsUOM2_L@60010 : Record 8053025;
    BEGIN
      // --> APP08-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // To return the quantity on purchase order for one line of blanket order
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Input:
      //   - DocNo_P = no. of document of the blanket order
      //   - LineNo_P = no. of document of the blanket order line
      // Output:
      //   - QtdOnBlanketOrder_R

      QtdOnBlanketOrder_R := 0;
      CLEAR(recPurchLine_L);
      recPurchLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Blanket Order No.","Blanket Order Line No.");
      recPurchLine_L.SETRANGE("Blanket Order No.",DocNo_P);
      recPurchLine_L.SETRANGE("Blanket Order Line No.",LineNo_P);

      IF recBlanketPurchLine_L.GET(recBlanketPurchLine_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order",DocNo_P,LineNo_P) THEN;
      //IF recItem_L.GET(recBlanketPurchLine_L."No.") THEN;

      IF recPurchLine_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          IF (recPurchLine_L."No." <> recBlanketPurchLine_L."No.")
                AND (recPurchLine_L.Type = recPurchLine_L.Type::Item)
                AND (recBlanketPurchLine_L.Type = recBlanketPurchLine_L.Type::Item) THEN BEGIN

            CLEAR(recUnitUOM_L);
            recUnitUOM_L.SETRANGE("Item No.",recPurchLine_L."No.");
            recUnitUOM_L.SETRANGE(Code,recBlanketPurchLine_L."Unit of Measure Code");
            IF NOT recUnitUOM_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              CLEAR(recCMCUnitsUOM_L);
              recCMCUnitsUOM_L.SETRANGE(Type,recCMCUnitsUOM_L.Type::Weight);
              recCMCUnitsUOM_L.SETRANGE(Code,recBlanketPurchLine_L."Unit of Measure Code");
              IF recCMCUnitsUOM_L.FINDFIRST THEN
                recUnitUOM_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := recCMCUnitsUOM_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure";

            CLEAR(recCMCUnitsUOM2_L);
            recCMCUnitsUOM2_L.SETRANGE(Type,recCMCUnitsUOM2_L.Type::Weight);
            recCMCUnitsUOM2_L.SETRANGE(Code,recPurchLine_L."Unit of Measure Code");
            IF recCMCUnitsUOM2_L.FINDFIRST THEN;

              QtdOnBlanketOrder_R := QtdOnBlanketOrder_R +  ROUND(recPurchLine_L."Outstanding Qty. (Base)"
                       * recCMCUnitsUOM2_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure" / recCMCUnitsUOM_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure",0.00001);


            END
            ELSE BEGIN

              QtdOnBlanketOrder_R := QtdOnBlanketOrder_R +  ROUND(recPurchLine_L."Outstanding Qty. (Base)"
                         * recUnitUOM_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure",0.00001);

            END;

          END
          ELSE
            QtdOnBlanketOrder_R := QtdOnBlanketOrder_R + recPurchLine_L."Outstanding Qty. (Base)";
        UNTIL recPurchLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      EXIT(QtdOnBlanketOrder_R);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateBrokerageLines@60002();
    VAR
      "<<-- 3LI LOCALS -->>"@60001 : Integer;
      BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L@60000 : Record 60031;
    BEGIN
      // 20150119-CER30-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
      // This function will update records on the table 60031
      // Input
      // Output
      IF "Document Type" <> "Document Type"::Invoice THEN
        EXIT;

      CLEAR(BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L);
      BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Invoice No.");
      BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L.SETRANGE("Invoice No.","Document No.");
      BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L.SETRANGE("Invoice Line No","Line No.");

        BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L."Unit cost" := "Direct Unit Cost";
        BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L.VALIDATE("Invoiced Qty",Quantity);
        BrokerageInvScheLlinesRec_L.MODIFY;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FctSetfilterAndTest@1000000000(VAR BooIsExctracted_P@1000000001 : Boolean;VAR BooRfcExist_P@1000000000 : Boolean;VAR RecECDLedgerEntry_P@1000000002 : Record 8053088);
    BEGIN
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // this fonction set filter on RFC ledger entry we need to create new RFC on ivoice
      // and set some usefull boolean value to know in wich case we are
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Input:
      //   - BooIsExctracted_P   : Are we in invoice/credit memo in extraction ?
      //   - BooRfcExist_P       : Some RFC entries exist on Receipt ?
      //   - RecECDLedgerEntry_P : REC use to read RFC ledger entry
      // Output:
      //   - BooIsExctracted_P
      //   - BooRfcExist_P
      //   - RecECDLedgerEntry_P


      // --> 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01
      BooIsExctracted_P := FALSE;
      BooRfcExist_P := FALSE;
      IF ((("Document Type"="Document Type"::Invoice) AND ("Receipt No." <> '')) OR
        (("Document Type"="Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND ("Return Shipment No." <> '')))
        THEN BEGIN
          BooIsExctracted_P := TRUE;

          RecECDLedgerEntry_P.RESET;
          RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
          RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type",RecECDLedgerEntry_P."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
          // --> 20160129-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2061-CJY01
          RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETFILTER("Discount Code",'<>%1','');
          // <-- 20160129-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2061-CJY01
          IF "Receipt No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecECDLedgerEntry_P."Document Type"::"Purchase Receipt");
            RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document No.","Receipt No.");
            RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Line No.","Receipt Line No.");

          END
          ELSE BEGIN
            RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecECDLedgerEntry_P."Document Type"::"Purchase Return Shipment");
            RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document No.","Return Shipment No.");
            RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Line No.","Return Shipment Line No.");
          END;
          IF NOT RecECDLedgerEntry_P.ISEMPTY THEN
            BooRfcExist_P := TRUE;
      END;
      // <-- 20151112-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY01

      // --> 20151120-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY02
      IF "Document Type"="Document Type"::"Return Order" THEN BEGIN
         IF (("Doc Type Extraction" <> "Doc Type Extraction"::" ") AND ("No. Doc Extraction" <> '') AND
            ("No. Line Doc Extraction" <> 0))
            THEN BEGIN
                IF "Doc Type Extraction" = "Doc Type Extraction"::Receipt THEN BEGIN
                  BooIsExctracted_P := TRUE;
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.RESET;
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type",RecECDLedgerEntry_P."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecECDLedgerEntry_P."Document Type"::"Purchase Receipt");
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document No.","No. Doc Extraction");
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Line No.","No. Line Doc Extraction");
                  // --> 20160129-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2661-CJY01
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETFILTER("Discount Code",'<>%1','');
                  // <-- 20160129-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2661-CJY01
                  IF NOT RecECDLedgerEntry_P.ISEMPTY THEN
                    BooRfcExist_P := TRUE;
                END;

                IF "Doc Type Extraction" = "Doc Type Extraction"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
                  BooIsExctracted_P := TRUE;
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.RESET;
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type",RecECDLedgerEntry_P."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecECDLedgerEntry_P."Document Type"::"Purchase Invoice");
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document No.","No. Doc Extraction");
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETRANGE("Document Line No.","No. Line Doc Extraction");
                  // --> 20160129-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2661-CJY01
                  RecECDLedgerEntry_P.SETFILTER("Discount Code",'<>%1','');
                  // <-- 20160129-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2661-CJY01
                  IF NOT RecECDLedgerEntry_P.ISEMPTY THEN
                    BooRfcExist_P := TRUE;
                END;


            END;
      END;
      // <-- 20151120-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2025-CJY02
    END;

    PROCEDURE FctItemSpotActive@1000000001();
    VAR
      PurchHeader_L@1000000000 : Record 38;
      ItemLedgerEntry_L@1000000001 : Record 32;
      PurchLine_L@1000000002 : Record 39;
    BEGIN
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // This function verify if Item exist on purchase order or in item ledger entry
      // linked with blanket purchase ordre
      //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Input:
      //
      // Output:

      // --> 20160414-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2937-CJY01

      IF Type <> Type::Item THEN
        EXIT;
      IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Blanket Order" THEN BEGIN
        IF PurchHeader_L.GET(PurchHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order","Document No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF PurchHeader_L."Specific Blanket Order" THEN BEGIN
            //Controle existance 'criture valid'e issue d'une commande issue de ce contrat
            ItemLedgerEntry_L.RESET;
            ItemLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Purch. Blanket Order No.","Document No.");
            ItemLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item No.","No.");
            ItemLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code","Variant Code");
            ItemLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Entry Type",ItemLedgerEntry_L."Entry Type"::Purchase);
            IF ItemLedgerEntry_L.FINDSET THEN
              ERROR(Text60002,"No.","Document No.");

            //Controle existance ligne commande achat issue de ce contrat
            PurchLine_L.RESET;
            PurchLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",PurchLine_L."Document Type"::Order);
            PurchLine_L.SETRANGE("Blanket Order No.","Document No.");
            PurchLine_L.SETRANGE("No.","No.");
            IF PurchLine_L.FINDSET THEN
              ERROR(Text60001,"No.","Document No.",PurchLine_L."Document No.");
          END;
        END;
      END;

      // <-- 20160414-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2937-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdatePurchaseLineShortcutDim@100000001();
    VAR
      ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L@100000000 : Record 5805;
      ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L@100000005 : Record 5805;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20210127-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
      IF ("Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" = '') OR ("Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" = '') THEN BEGIN
        ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.RESET;
        ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
        ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
        ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.","Line No.");
        // --> 20210310-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
        ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.SETRANGE("Affect Charge On Filter", TRUE);
        IF ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.ISEMPTY THEN
          ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.SETRANGE("Affect Charge On Filter");
        // <-- 20210310-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
        IF ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.RESET;
          ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
          ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
          ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.","Line No.");
          // --> 20210310-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
          ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.SETRANGE("Affect Charge On Filter", ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L."Affect Charge On Filter");
          // <-- 20210310-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
          IF "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
            ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.SETFILTER("Location Code", '<>%1', ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L."Location Code");
            IF NOT ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L."Location Code";
              ValidateShortcutDimCode(1,"Shortcut Dimension 1 Code");
              MODIFY;
            END;
            // --> 20210310-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
            ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.SETRANGE("Location Code");
            // <-- 20210310-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
          END;
          IF "Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
            ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.SETFILTER("Product Code", '<>%1', ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L."Product Code");
            IF NOT ItemChargeAssgntPurch2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              "Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := ItemChargeAssgntPurch_L."Product Code";
              ValidateShortcutDimCode(2,"Shortcut Dimension 2 Code");
              MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20210127-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3491-MRU01
    END;
